# Constructivity audit

Project declarations audited: 1920

## Trusted primitives (`@[cprim]`)

* `BezoutCounterexample.Constructive.cotrans` : `∀ {x y : ℝ}, x < y → ∀ (z : ℝ), x < z ∨ z < y`
* `BezoutCounterexample.Constructive.exists_fixedPoint_half` : `∀ {E : Type u_1} [inst : NormedAddCommGroup E] [CompleteSpace E] (Φ : E → E) (x₀ : E) (r : ℝ),
  0 ≤ r →
    (∀ x ∈ Metric.closedBall x₀ r, Φ x ∈ Metric.closedBall x₀ r) →
      (∀ x ∈ Metric.closedBall x₀ r, ∀ x' ∈ Metric.closedBall x₀ r, 2 * ‖Φ x - Φ x'‖ ≤ ‖x - x'‖) →
        ∃ x ∈ Metric.closedBall x₀ r, Φ x = x`
* `BezoutCounterexample.Constructive.exists_inv_pos` : `∀ {x : ℝ}, 0 < x → ∃ y, 0 < y ∧ x * y = 1`
* `BezoutCounterexample.Constructive.exists_isGcd_polynomial_field` : `∀ (K : Type u_1) [inst : Field K],
  (∀ (x : K), x = 0 ∨ x ≠ 0) → ∀ (f g : Polynomial K), ∃ h, BezoutCounterexample.Constructive.IsGcd h f g`
* `BezoutCounterexample.Constructive.exists_root_pos` : `∀ {x : ℝ}, 0 < x → ∀ {n : ℕ}, n ≠ 0 → ∃ y, 0 < y ∧ y ^ n = x`
* `BezoutCounterexample.Constructive.le_of_not_lt'` : `∀ {a b : ℝ}, ¬b < a → a ≤ b`
* `BezoutCounterexample.Constructive.sign_split` : `∀ {x c : ℝ}, 0 < c → 1 ≤ c * |x| → 0 < x ∨ x < 0`
* `BezoutCounterexample.Constructive.uniqueChoice` : `{α : Sort u_1} → {P : α → Prop} → (∃! x, P x) → { x // P x }`
